{"type": "system", "content": "You write one provider's Chift invoicing connector. Chift's test suite (/tests/) is the only judge.\n\nThe first message holds the contract, what each Chift test file checks, and Chift's target schemas.\nFind provider operations with list_operations, read them with describe_operation (describe_schema\nfor a `$ref`), and inspect real records with call_api (GET only). Batch independent tool calls;\nrepair with edit_file; do not browse generator internals or look for other connectors.\n\n1. Pick the operationIds for the six contract methods and call generate_client. Never edit\n   client.py.\n2. Write connector.py: `class Connector(InvoicingConnector)` from chift.contract, the six methods\n   with exactly its parameters, using only the generated client. The base stores self.client.\n3. run_tests, fix, repeat until it passes. Then stop and reply with your report (never a file).\n\nNever edit Chift's code, tests or specs, and never special-case ids, names or amounts from test\noutput.\n\nMapping rules:\n- Map concepts, not field names; look for a concept under another name before calling it unmapped.\n  Return every Chift field, or list it in `UNMAPPED = {schema: {field: reason}}`.\n  A fact true of every record is a value, not a gap: if the provider's entity is always a customer,\n  map is_customer=True, is_supplier=False, is_prospect=False, and accept those values on create.\n- Money: scale by the ISO 4217 exponent (never a hard-coded 100), both ways; refuse extra precision\n  and unknown currencies.\n- Statuses: map every documented value explicitly, and put the provider's own description of\n  each value in a comment next to it; place it by that description, not its name. `draft` = not\n  yet issued (prepared, awaiting approval, accumulating); `posted` = issued, not fully paid\n  (incl. overdue, written off); `paid` = fully settled; `cancelled` = voided or replaced. An\n  unmapped value read back raises KeyError (`MAP[value]`), never falls back to a status or type.\n- A value the provider did not send stays None: required fields as `data[\"key\"]`, optional as\n  `data.get(\"key\")`. A value you cannot map raises (`MAP[value]`, or `Unsupported`). Never write\n  a fallback in any spelling (`.get(k, \"x\")`, `x or \"x\"`, `v if c else \"x\"`, `if v is None:\n  v = \"x\"`) and never invent a placeholder like \"Unnamed\": when /tests/test_code_rules.py names\n  a line, remove the substitute value, do not respell it.\n- Translate, never validate or recompute: the provider owns totals, so return what it stored.\n  Send what the caller sent; leave an unsent field out rather than choosing a value. If the\n  provider can't represent a value, raise `Unsupported` before any write (a raise after a create\n  leaves a record behind). A discount may be a coupon that can target a line; check the whole\n  create schema before declining.\n- When no provider field means the same thing, decline with `Unsupported`. A skipped test is a\n  correct, honest result; a passing test bought with a workaround is cheating and is worse than any\n  skip. Never keep markers, copies or originals in properties, metadata, notes or custom fields,\n  and never put a value in a field that means something else, to make a read return what was sent.\n- A person's name never goes in company_name. Keep billing vs delivery addresses. Walk cursors for\n  numbered pages. Convert dates and datetimes with `datetime`, not string slicing or concatenation.\n- Keep it short: one-to-one field maps and declined fields as tables, not a block per field.\n- Put a `# Mapping decision:` comment on each mapping table and judgment call; add `REVIEW:` when\n  unsure.\n\nUse only the standard library, httpx and iso4217. Write only connector.py. The\nconnection is a test account: use example.com emails and draft invoices.\n"}
{"type": "human", "content": "Generate the hyperline connector: write /providers/hyperline/generated/connector.py (the folder starts empty; logs/ and report.md are the harness's). You may read the repository except .env files. You have 60 work calls, then one tool-free final report.\nMoney: `from iso4217 import Currency`; `Currency(\"EUR\").exponent` is 2, JPY 0; `Decimal(str(value)).scaleb(exponent)`.\n\nThe contract you implement (/chift/contract.py):\n```python\n\"\"\"The interface every generated connector implements. The app calls nothing else.\n\nA connector is `class Connector(InvoicingConnector)` in `providers/<name>/generated/connector.py`.\nPython refuses to build one that misses a method, and defining one whose method takes different\nparameters fails at import, naming the method: the interface cannot drift.\n\nDicts, not models: each one is an instance of the named schema in Chift's OpenAPI\n(`chift/openapi.yaml`), and the acceptance suite validates them against it.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport inspect\nfrom abc import ABC, abstractmethod\n\n\nclass Unsupported(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Raise when the provider cannot represent what the caller asked for.\n\n    The app answers Chift's 400 with \"Not supported by this provider: \" followed by this message,\n    so pass only the reason, e.g. `Unsupported(\"contact phone: Hyperline customers have no phone\")`.\n    Declining is always better than storing something different from what the caller sent, such as\n    dropping a discount.\n    \"\"\"\n\n\nclass InvoicingConnector(ABC):\n    \"\"\"Built once as `Connector(client)`, with the provider's generated, authenticated client.\n\n    - Every field of Chift's output schemas is returned (null when a record has no value), or\n      declared in the connector module's `UNMAPPED = {schema: {field: reason}}`, e.g.\n      `{\"ContactItemOut\": {\"phone\": \"Hyperline customers have no phone number\"}}`. Schemas:\n      ContactItemOut, AddressItemOutInvoicing, InvoiceItemOutSingle, InvoiceLineItemOut.\n    - Chift's `id` is the provider's record id, so get-one works with any listed id; `source_ref`\n      is `{\"id\": <provider id>, \"model\": <provider entity name>}`.\n    - One record read through a list, through get-one and returned by a create maps to the same\n      dict (invoice `lines` excepted: lists may leave them empty).\n    - Provider errors (`httpx.HTTPStatusError`) propagate unchanged: the app turns a provider 404\n      into Chift's 404 and any other provider failure into 502.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, client) -> None:\n        self.client = client\n\n    def __init_subclass__(cls, **kwargs) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Fail at import if an implemented method takes other parameters than the contract's.\"\"\"\n        super().__init_subclass__(**kwargs)\n        for name, method in vars(InvoicingConnector).items():\n            if getattr(method, \"__isabstractmethod__\", False) and name in vars(cls):\n                expected = list(inspect.signature(method).parameters)\n                actual = list(inspect.signature(vars(cls)[name]).parameters)\n                if actual != expected:\n                    raise TypeError(f\"{cls.__name__}.{name} takes {actual}, the contract {expected}\")\n\n    @abstractmethod\n    def get_contact(self, contact_id: str) -> dict:\n        \"\"\"A Chift `ContactItemOut`.\"\"\"\n\n    @abstractmethod\n    def list_contacts(self, page: int, size: int) -> tuple[list[dict], int]:\n        \"\"\"Page `page` (from 1) of `size` (1..100) `ContactItemOut`s, and the total count.\n\n        A page past the end is an empty list with the true total.\n        \"\"\"\n\n    @abstractmethod\n    def create_contact(self, body: dict) -> dict:\n        \"\"\"Create from a validated Chift `ContactItemIn`; return the `ContactItemOut`.\n\n        `body` holds only the fields the caller sent.\n        \"\"\"\n\n    @abstractmethod\n    def get_invoice(self, invoice_id: str) -> dict:\n        \"\"\"A Chift `InvoiceItemOutSingle`, with its lines.\"\"\"\n\n    @abstractmethod\n    def list_invoices(self, page: int, size: int) -> tuple[list[dict], int]:\n        \"\"\"Page `page` (from 1) of `size` (1..100) `InvoiceItemOut`s, and the total count.\n\n        A page past the end is an empty list with the true total.\n        \"\"\"\n\n    @abstractmethod\n    def create_invoice(self, body: dict) -> dict:\n        \"\"\"Create from a validated Chift `InvoiceItemIn`; return the `InvoiceItemOut`.\n\n        `body` holds only the fields the caller sent.\n        \"\"\"\n```\n\nChift's acceptance suite, which decides success:\n- /tests/test_code_rules.py: The connector never replaces a missing value with a plausible one.\n- /tests/test_contacts.py: Contacts: create one, retrieve one, retrieve all.\n- /tests/test_fields.py: Every Chift field is either mapped or declared unmapped with a reason. Nothing goes missing.\n- /tests/test_invoices.py: Invoices: create one, retrieve one, retrieve all. Amounts to the cent.\n- /tests/test_pagination.py: Chift's numbered pages, whatever the provider paginates with underneath.\n\nChift's target schemas (from
